# Representative logo inventory

This document defines the versioned representative inventory required by PLAN.md R26.
It records the selection rules, record schema, legal handling, and coverage accounting
so that document sourcing can proceed without changing the rules midstream.
No selection is claimed until documents are actually inventoried below.

## Contained bitmap-edge checkpoint (2026-10-07)

SG Rotation Leipzig (`inv-012`) becomes exact in all nine frozen variants on
Windows and Linux. The current cohort has **24/27 exact documents**, marks
**12/13**, wordmarks **12/14**: **237 exact, 21 known and zero rejected** variants.
Only its three large one-pixel differences change; all 234 earlier exact corporate
PNGs and every other complete comparison remain. Sources, options, browser
references and provenance stay frozen. SimpleLogin, Zeppelin and IBM remain.

The expanded bank has 1,234 fixtures (1,148 exact, 62 known, 24 rejected), 36
research probes (22 exact, 13 known, one rejected) and 92 Default renders:
1,362 executions with identical complete Windows/Linux outcomes and PNG bytes.
All 1,167 earlier exact fixture/research PNGs remain. One Default PNG changes
without a matching-options claim. Three new untuned solid-path controls become
exact from 20–21 differing pixels; gradient twins retain 230 RGB-only differences
each after their alpha errors disappear. Eighteen captures are stable, healthy
and cleaned. The follow-up geometry was selected before its first comparison,
after the production prototype. [PLAN](../PLAN.md) owns the current queue;
[the design record](RASTERIZATION_DESIGN.md#contained-bitmap-edge-construction-2026-10-07)
records mechanism and limits. Earlier checkpoint totals below remain historical.

## Linux platform-reference supplement (2026-10-07)

Local Linux renders reproduce all 1,362 Windows bank outcomes and PNG bytes.
Across the frozen corporate matrix, 255/258 candidate PNGs are identical to Windows.
Pepsi's three small backgrounds differ in 22 RGB-only pixels each, but match
the same pinned Chrome version/revision on Linux in nine healthy, cleaned,
stable fresh captures. The [supplement](../tests/References/inventory/corporate-cohort-linux64.json)
keeps the original Windows freeze, inputs, options and denominator. Both platforms
pass the cohort tests; Linux uses the supplement for those three variants.
The complete fresh Linux-browser matrix now covers all 258 frozen variants with
774 stable, healthy, cleaned captures. Of the reference PNGs, 255 match Windows
bytes, while all nine new small Pepsi captures reproduce the existing supplement.
The Linux result is 237 exact variants, 21 known differences, zero rejections and
the same 24/27 exact documents, marks 12/13 and wordmarks 12/14. No failure or
unmeasured variant is omitted. Remote CI remains open; detailed evidence stays
under ignored `artifacts/linux-delivery-20261007/`.

92 legacy-bank files render. The four prior rejections (`inv-007`, `inv-012`,

rendered PNG was unchanged at that checkpoint. Renderability is separate from fidelity. The historical

establish completion of every declared size/background or current corporate-logo
coverage. Fifty-seven inventory headings identify flags or flag badges/icons,
so this whole inventory is not the representative corporate denominator.

witnesses. Generic numeric reading matches all 436 authored points and the actual
large CTM. Independent native density replay closes all ten lower-row residuals
using 254/182 raw path points rather than 480/421 flattened samples. Its adapter
limitations and original input identity are in
[the density record](../tests/References/inventory/inv-002-native-density.json).
General tile-local/event and affine/quad/convex/stroke/layer parity remain open.

Twelve fresh mapping configurations from four CC0 sources have 36 healthy stable
captures and are exact. The updated portable bank executes **818 cases**:
**660 fixtures = 534 exact + 120 known renders + six rejections**, across
396 base documents / 264 variants; 66 research = 65 known renders / one rejection;
and 92 Default inventory inputs. Original references are retained. These synthetic
counts establish neither an all-variant corporate checkpoint nor a representative
corporate rate. Earlier batch counts below remain dated history. [PLAN](../PLAN.md)
owns the current work order; the design and benchmark records retain mechanism,
mixed changes and delivery/resource evidence.

## Purpose and non-goals

The inventory steers implementation toward ordinary, static, self-contained corporate
SVG logos, including outlined wordmarks. It does not replace the fixture corpus in
tests/Fixtures/manifest.json, which remains the authoritative fixed-inventory catalog
for admitted behavior with byte hashes and renderer verification.
Synthetic probes stay in their own count and must never stand in for real-export coverage.

## Selection rules

Documents are independently selected real export outputs, not constructed around the
admitted subset. The initial set targets 20 to 30 documents, growing toward 50 to 100.
Selection covers these archetypes, including failing cases:

- Flat curved marks and outlined wordmarks with counters and many contours.
- Holes, even-odd and nonzero winding.
- Rotations and non-uniform transforms.
- Thin strokes, hairlines, dashes and round or square caps and joins.
- Linear and radial gradients, single and multiple stops, transparency.
- Clipped and translucent combinations, groups with opacity, masks.
- Text as paths (outlined wordmarks need no fonts); live text stays out of scope.
- Small, intended and large render sizes across transparent, white and black backgrounds.

## Record schema

Each inventoried document carries a stable ID plus:

- `source`: where the export came from (tool, exporter version when known).
- `license`: rights status with evidence; restricted assets stay private (see below).
- `retrievalDate`: ISO date of acquisition.
- `archetypes`: list of archetype tags from the selection rules above.
- `variants`: size and background variants declared up front for aggregation.
- `status`: untested, exact, known-difference, rejects or unsupported with reason.
- `reference`: pinned oracle capture identity once measured, never a candidate render.

## Legal handling

Every asset needs provenance before admission. Contributor-original work follows the
corpus CC0 SPDX declaration convention. Restricted assets stay private: they are
never committed, never embedded in fixtures, and any local-only overlay directory
remains untracked. Do not simplify logos or redraw them to fit the renderer.

## Operating notes

Procedures established sourcing batches 1-11; follow them for later batches.

- Sourcing: verify LicenseShortName=Public domain via the Commons API extmetadata against the true file page (titles from memory go stale; confirm size and SHA-1 against local bytes before recording). Record trademark disclosure where depicted marks suggest it. Hold back redundant same-category rejects once a category has several data points. Check the entry list for the same Commons file or subject before downloading (2026-09-26: batches 14 and 17 re-sourced inv-022 and inv-021 byte-identically; both withdrawn).
- Isolation: candidates parse as XML with no script/foreignObject/animate/image/use-external content and no http hrefs; they live only in tests/Fixtures/candidates/ with provenance, carry no CC0 SPDX header, never enter tests/Fixtures/original/, and must stay unreferenced by project files and the NuGet package (verify with a pack listing zero .svg).
- Hashes: record the canonical LF blob SHA-256 (repo policy normalizes line endings on commit, so working-copy hashes of CRLF upstream files go stale; always re-verify against git show after committing).
- Oracle triples: one sequential fresh Chrome process at a time via capture-chrome.ps1 with absolute paths (pwsh, not powershell -File); three fresh-process runs must be byte-identical before recording; record viewport, hash and the full pin identity (Chrome for Testing 153.0.8010.52, Chromium 1681091, capture recipe 3, win64).
- Known differences: reproduce the VisualDiff numbers from committed bytes and retain candidate identity hashes per variant (R27: the tuple alone is not identity).
- After any admission commit, re-run the attribute scan (root x/y, xml:space, enable-background, root class, defs attributes, shape clip-rule) over candidates/ and re-probe exactly the files carrying newly admitted attributes; update their statuses. Feature commits update docs/SUPPORT_MATRIX.md in the same commit.

## Coverage accounting

Apply the R27 lessons: aggregate exactness over every declared variant, never simplify
the denominator to improve the score, and freeze the inventory and target before
any most-logos claim per S17. Entry counts must not stand in for all-variant success.
Render every entry through the manifest-style gate: asserted rejection with category
when rejected, retained reference with measured mismatch when renderable but wrong.

## Selected documents (batch 1, 2026-09-26)

Public-domain logo SVGs sourced from Wikimedia Commons (all LicenseShortName=Public domain per Commons API extmetadata, 2026-09-26; no Trademarked template on file pages; trusted revisions). Commons PD tags state copyright status; depicted marks may carry trademark considerations, recorded here rather than hidden. Files live in tests/Fixtures/candidates/ with provenance; third-party files carry no CC0 SPDX header and are never copied into tests/Fixtures/original/. Repo policy (*.svg text eol=lf) normalizes line endings on commit: recorded hashes identify the canonical LF blob bytes, which is what any fresh checkout materializes. (XML parsing normalizes line endings before parsing, so oracle captures and renderer probes are unaffected.)
